# Liber-Tea-Enjoyer's Liber-Tea-Edition v0.4.1

<img width="2000" height="1125" alt="Liber-Tea-Edition v0 4 1 - header picture" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b85667ac-00e5-4e8c-81a9-1ec6324441bf" />


> ## HELLFORGE ONLY
> **Liber-Tea-Edition v0.4.1 runs on HellForge, the offline copy of Helldivers 2 (game build 1.8.46015). It does not run on the current live game.**
> Tested with HellForge v0.3.0-beta (added on its Mods page) and Bingus Shared Loader v20.
> Not for HD2 Arsenal or HD2 Mod Manager: this version has not been tested with either (they install mods into the live game).

**Liber-Tea-Edition (LTEd) is a full rebalance of Helldivers 2 with editors for every number.** Weapons, stratagems, armour passives,
enemies and spawns are rebalanced, and boxes inside the game's own menus show the numbers of whatever you highlight and let you change
them yourself, with a controller or a mouse. Every change can be switched back to the game's own numbers with one press.

**Fixed in v0.4.1:** the SECONDARIES, THROWABLES and BOOSTER boxes now show on HellForge v0.3.0-beta, the armour camos now paint your
helmet, body armour and cape, and the welcome window, the after-mission screen and the milestone pop-ups press with A, SPACE or ENTER (on
the ship R3 opens the game's pause menu).

Problems we know about are marked **KNOWN ISSUE** right under the feature they belong to, and what waits on HellForge itself is marked
**WAITING ON HELLFORGE**.

**How to download:** on this page open **Releases** (right side), then under **Assets** click
`Liber-Tea-Enjoyer's Liber-Tea-Edition v0.4.1.zip`. That zip is the mod; you do not need the "Source code" files.

---

## What you need

- **HellForge** (v0.3.0-beta), set up with its copy of the game.
- **Bingus Shared Loader v20** (LTEd runs through it; keep it on).
- Optional: **Vanilla Plus Megapack v42** (Bingus's pack). LTEd works alongside it, every option on, and its passives box gets buttons
  for Vanilla Plus's Match Your Colors. Run only ONE Vanilla Plus: if you add Bingus's pack, switch HellForge's own bundled Vanilla Plus off.
- Optional: **Hell Forge Difficulty** (bundled with HellForge) can stay ON; LTEd knows difficulty 11.

## Install (HellForge)

1. Close the game (HellForge locks the mod switches while a session runs).
2. Start HellForge.exe and open the **Mods** page.
3. Click **Add mods...** and pick the Bingus Shared Loader v20 zip (skip this if you have it).
4. Click **Add mods...** again and pick `Liber-Tea-Enjoyer's Liber-Tea-Edition v0.4.1.zip`.
5. Make sure both switches are ON. If an older Liber-Tea-Edition is in the list, remove it or switch it off (only one version can run).
6. Click the Liber-Tea-Edition row to see its 15 options (one per part, see below); leave them all ticked for the full mod.
7. Start a session from the launcher as usual. The first time you reach the ship, a welcome window shows where everything is.

(Instead of Add mods... you can copy the zip into `Documents\HellForge\mods\`; the Mods page shows it at once.)

When you start the game you will be greeted by the welcome window. You can choose to play with my changes to the game which deliver on the game's promise: Overpowered Weapons Against Overwhelming Odds. All weapons are buffed to perfection while all enemy spawns are ramped up by 3x, along with many other changes to every aspect of the game. If you do not wish to play the Liber-Tea-Edition of the game and wish to make your own changes from scratch, you can click "Play with Vanilla Settings" and you have the Liber-Tea to make all your own changes. Play the way you want.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Welcome Window" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/226a16a2-4fc3-47ed-8fee-6a320bfea6ab" />


## The 15 parts of LTEd

Each part is one option of the mod on HellForge's Mods page: untick a part and start a new session to turn it off. The shared core
rides inside every part, so any mix works.

PASSIVES, PRIMARIES, SECONDARIES, THROWABLES, SUPPORT WEAPONS, BACKPACKS, SENTRIES + EMPLACEMENTS, EAGLES, ORBITALS, VEHICLES, CHARACTER,
SPAWNS, XENOBIOLOGY, MISSIONS, PROGRESSION.

## Where to find everything

| where | what |
|---|---|
| Armory > Weaponry | the box beside the list shows and edits the highlighted primary, secondary or throwable; more numbers under ADVANCED STATS EDITING |
| Armory > Weaponry > a gun > Customize | the attachment editor, any attachment on any gun, and the LIBER-TEA-EDITION WEAPON CAMOS button |
| Armory > Armory | the passives box: pick and edit the passives of each body armour, helmet and cape |
| Armory > Booster | pick a booster: its numbers show under its description |
| Armory > Character | EDIT CHARACTER STATS, TOGGLE CHANGES, BESTIARY, ENEMY EDITS, SPAWN EDITS, the MISSION EDITOR (host only), PROGRESSION |
| Ship Management > Stratagems | the stratagems box shows and edits the highlighted stratagem; SHOW HIDDEN STRATAGEMS in the bottom bar |
| Ship Management > Vehicle Customization | LIBER-TEA-EDITION CAMOS for every vehicle, Pelican-1 and the hellpod |
| The pause menu, in a mission | the IN-MISSION EDITOR |

## Controls

- **Controller:** the right stick moves the highlight; a short tap of R3 presses, opens, picks or grabs a number. A grabbed number: right /
  left a step, up / down a big step; tap R3 again to let go.
- **Mouse:** click, scroll, or click a number and type it.
- **Keyboard:** W A S D move, Enter presses.
- **The welcome window, the after-mission screen and the milestone pop-ups** (all on the ship, where R3 opens the game's pause menu): **A,
  SPACE or ENTER** presses the highlighted button; the stick or W A S D moves the highlight; in the two progression windows LB / RB turn
  the pages and B closes. Each of these windows names its keys on screen.
- **SHOW VANILLA STATS:** hold it (R3 or the mouse button) to see the game's own numbers in place.
- Every change works at once and is saved. Every box has **BACK** and **CLOSE** at its top right, and a **HIDE / SHOW** button to get it
  out of the way.

---

## Features

### The rebalance

Every weapon class has a job and a weakness, ordinary guns can deal with tougher enemies without turning everything into explosives, and
there is more ammo for more enemies. Every stat LTEd changes is listed at the end of this page, item by item, with the game's number and
LTEd's.

### A box for every item, and every number editable

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Primary" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/08ad26ce-16a2-4d60-b51c-a6b9692d0348" />

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Secondary" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/c6a77328-a1a6-4155-8524-f642e35dc7e4" />

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Throwable" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/8738a977-50d5-4b74-8583-b17f0bb98db1" />

- **Weapons** (Armory > Weaponry): highlight a primary, secondary or throwable and its box shows its numbers: damage, durable damage,
  armour penetration on all four angles, magazine and spare ammo, fire rate, handling, reload (from empty and partial, on guns that have
  both), status effects and more. **Every number the box shows can be changed**, not only the ones LTEd changes. **ADVANCED STATS EDITING**
  opens every other number the item has. A number shared with other items asks first and names them.
- **A SOUND line** on every weapon: how loud it is and how far enemies hear it; it changes with the fitted attachments and ammo.
- **RESET THIS ... TO DEFAULT** puts one item back; your edits are saved between sessions.

**WAITING ON HELLFORGE:** the G-11 Caltrops (Scope of Authority warbond) is inside HellForge's copy of the game but stays locked:
HellForge's item list was made before it came out, so even Profile > Account sync does not give it to its owners, and HellForge's mod kit
has no ownership check, so LTEd does not unlock a paid item for everyone. If HellForge's item list gains it, LTEd's box shows and edits its
numbers like any other throwable.

**KNOWN ISSUE (the cause is being checked):** on 20 guns LTEd's magazine numbers sit on the gun's magazine part rather than on the gun
itself, and in some games such a gun may keep the game's own numbers (the P-19 Redeemer has shown its normal 31 rounds instead of LTEd's
75). The guns: the magazine size of the P-19 Redeemer, P-2 Peacemaker, AR-32 Pacifier and SG-225SP Breaker Spray&Pray, and the spare
magazines of the P-19 Redeemer, P-2 Peacemaker, SG-225SP Breaker Spray&Pray, AR-23 Liberator, AR-23A Liberator Carbine, AR-23C Liberator
Concussive, AR-23P Liberator Penetrator, AR-59 Suppressor, SG-8P Punisher Plasma, R-4 Hyena, R-72 Censor, MP-98 Knight, SMG-203 Gallant,
SMG-37 Defender, SMG-72 Pummeler, SMG/FLAM-34 Stoker, SG-225 Breaker, SG-225IE Breaker Incendiary and PLAS-15 Loyalist.

**KNOWN ISSUE (being fixed in v0.4.2):** the SG-22 Bushwhacker (a secondary in the game) sits in LTEd's PRIMARIES part: on the SECONDARIES
list its box is the PRIMARIES box, and its changes go with PRIMARIES (its option on the Mods page and TOGGLE CHANGES), not SECONDARIES.

### Attachments (Armory > a gun > Customize)

- **The attachment editor:** pick each attachment and change its numbers.
- **Any attachment on any gun** (COMES WITH): give a gun any part as its default, for example a suppressor on a rifle that never had one.
- **REPLACE THIS PART WITH:** turn a part on a gun's own list into another part (it warns which other guns share that part).
- **Underbarrels on more guns:** give one of 14 guns the grenade launcher, the shotgun or the flamethrower underbarrel, and it gets the
  switch to fire it, like the One-Two, the Stoker and the Arbitrator.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Attachments" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/c27cb842-7e44-4c75-9022-677fda91d429" />

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Functional Underbarrel Options" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/a5acb81f-4cd4-4378-9e57-d658f578a2f3" />

**WAITING ON HELLFORGE:** HellForge's mod kit cannot add a part to a gun's own list in the game's Customize screen yet (it has no
attachment content type, and that list comes from HellForge's own item list). That is why COMES WITH changes the part a gun is built with
(the "-" setup) and REPLACE THIS PART WITH changes a part on every gun that uses it. With an attachment content type, parts could join a
gun's own list, sidearms' too, and be picked there like its own parts. We have asked SkyeShade, HellForge's maker, about it; if you would
like it too, let him know.

### Stratagems (Ship Management > Stratagems)

- A box for every support weapon, backpack, sentry, emplacement, Eagle, orbital and vehicle stratagem, the same way as the weapons.
- **SHOW HIDDEN STRATAGEMS** (the bottom bar) shows the mission-only stratagems so their numbers can be edited too.
- **EDIT ARMOR** on every vehicle (Exosuits, FRVs, the Bastion and the others): every part's health and armour, with the parts' pictures.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Stratagems" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/55da6001-fd37-43fb-9113-62ce93d96577" />

**KNOWN ISSUE (being fixed in v0.4.2):** in the stratagems box, ADVANCED STATS EDITING makes the box disappear instead of opening the
advanced numbers. Leave Ship Management and come back to get the box back.

### Switch it all back any time: TOGGLE CHANGES

- **TOGGLE CHANGES** (Armory > Character): put every part, or one part, back to the game's own numbers, and bring Liber-Tea-Edition back
  later. A pop-up asks whether to keep all, some or none of your own edits. You can also hide and bring back each part's boxes.
- On every LTEd change you can see what it does and put it back: each box shows VANILLA or ON / OFF lines for it.
- The welcome window asks the same question at the start: **PLAY WITH THE LIBER-TEA-EDITION CHANGES** or **PLAY WITH VANILLA SETTINGS**.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Toggle Changes" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b3f65c67-5556-48f2-9c58-819e72c1068c" />

**KNOWN ISSUE (being fixed in v0.4.2):** once you tick DO NOT SHOW AGAIN in the welcome window, nothing in the game brings it back yet
(BRING BACK ALL OVERLAYS does not). Until then: close the game, open `Liber-Tea-Edition-settings.log` in LTEd's folder (see Files it
writes) and delete the line `welcome.hide = yes`.

### Spawns: three times the enemies, or anything you like (SPAWN EDITS)

- **LTEd starts at three times the enemies:** patrols three times as often and as big, bug breaches and bot drops three times as often and
  as big, bigger outpost groups, enemies noticing you from three times as far, mission waves three times as often. **VANILLA** is one
  press away on every row, and TOGGLE CHANGES puts all of it back at once.
- Every enemy's spawn rate: OFF, X0.5, VANILLA, X2, X3, SURGE X5, X10.
- **ONLY** buttons per enemy family (for example only Voteless), with a picture of each variant to tick in or out.
- **CURATE ENEMIES:** per faction, tick which enemies turn up at all; the ones that call in drops and breaches stay on so the game keeps
  working.
- Spawn changes work in missions you host.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Individual Spawns" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/60e59a72-5a23-40a7-abf8-6d484709afb9" />

### The Mission Editor (MISSIONS, host only)

- Pick a faction and one, two or three mission types; **SEARCH** rerolls the operations of the planet open on the holotable until one
  matches, then you pick it on the war table.
- Side objectives: **AT LEAST ONE** or **NONE (VETO)** for each one; operation modifiers and enemy sub-factions as search filters.
- The MISSION TIMER and COMMANDO TIMER settings are here too.
- Friends who join need no mod for it.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Edit Missions" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/859798e9-27f0-42c9-bfea-3c05c5d229d7" />

**KNOWN ISSUE (being fixed in v0.4.2):** the editor still lets you pick things the game never puts together, and such a search ends with
NO MATCH IN 300 TRIES:
- a Terminid or Automaton operation holds at most two standard missions (its third is an ERADICATE mission or BLITZ: SEARCH AND DESTROY),
  and the same mission type twice comes only in Automaton city operations at difficulty 3-4;
- some mission types come only at some difficulties or on some planets: ACTIVATE OIL PUMPS only at difficulty 2-3, EXTRACT E-711 only on
  the Gloom's Hive Worlds;
- with Hell Forge Difficulty on, an AT LEAST ONE rule for LIDAR STATION, SEAF ARTILLERY, SPORE SPEWER or MOBILE RADAR can never be met:
  leave those on ANY.

"FOUND IN 0 TRIES" means the operation was already on your war table: open the same planet, set the same difficulty and pick the
operation the line names.

### Progression: mastery, titles and camo picks (PROGRESSION)

- **Weapon mastery:** every kill with a weapon counts (yours only). The more kills, the better it handles: ergonomics, sway, recoil,
  spread, reload and draw time improve step by step (throwables throw farther, melee weapons draw faster). Damage is never raised.
- **Titles** for enemy kills (for example CHARGER MURDERER at 100 Charger kills). Pick one with **SELECT TITLE** and wear it: it shows on
  LTEd's own screens (the PROGRESSION window, the TITLE page box and the mission report).

- **The PROGRESSION window** (Armory > Character) with every weapon's picture and mastery, an **after-mission screen** back on the ship,
  and **milestone pop-ups** (never during a mission).
- **Camo picks:** every mastery step of a weapon gives a camo pick for that weapon; every enemy kill step gives a pick for an armour piece,
  a sentry, a vehicle or a backpack. Unspent picks wait for you.

game yet (it has no title content type). If it gains one, the titles you earn could sit in the game's own TITLE list and on your player
card. We have asked SkyeShade, HellForge's maker, about it; if you would like it too, let him know.

### Camos

- **LTEd's own camos, made live in the game:** 30 kinds x 40 colour sets x endless variations; **RE-ROLL** for a new variation, pick the
  kind and the colours, and go back through the **last 10 patterns** you looked at. Camo picks are earned through the Progression System. 
- **Guns:** the **LIBER-TEA-EDITION WEAPON CAMOS** button on a gun's Customize page (where its PATTERNS are); the gun and its fitted
  attachments wear it in the Armory, on the ship and in missions.
- **Support weapons and other stratagem objects** (backpacks, sentries, emplacements, Eagle-1): the **LIBER-TEA-EDITION CAMOS** button in
  their stratagem box.

- **Vehicles, Pelican-1 and the hellpod:** **LIBER-TEA-EDITION CAMOS** on Ship Management's VEHICLE CUSTOMIZATION tab, with **COVERAGE**:
  MINIMALIST, NORMAL, HEAVY or COMPLETE (how much of the vehicle takes the camo).
- **Armour:** helmet, body armour and cape, through the same paint system Vanilla Plus's Match Your Colors uses: a piece you gave an LTEd
  camo wears it, every other piece keeps Match Your Colors.

**KNOWN ISSUE:** 7 weapons have no LTEd camo options yet: the AR-2 Coyote, R-4 Hyena, DBS-2 Double Freedom, FLAM-40 Flamethrower, MG-206
Heavy Machine Gun, MG-43 Machine Gun and RS-422 Railgun. Why: their painted parts carry only the kind of paint every gun shares, with no
camo slot of their own, so LTEd's camos have nothing of theirs to paint yet. We are working on a way to give them camos too.

### Passives: your helmet and cape count plus native transmog (PASSIVES)

- In the normal game only your body armour's passive does anything. Here **every helmet and cape carries the passive of its armour set**,
  and 1, 2 or 3 pieces with the same passive give stronger values; different passives add up (with a few ceilings).
- **The passives box** (Armory > Armory) shows what your set gives; the passive name of each piece is a drop-down to pick another one. Essentially this is a native version of transmog.
- **EDIT PASSIVE STATS:** every number of every passive at 1, 2 and 3 pieces.
- With Vanilla Plus's Match Your Colors: **COLOR MATCH**, **COLOR MATCH WEAPONS**, **CAPE COLOR** (OFF, ON, NO CAPE), MATERIALS, PAINT and
  RECOLOR HOODS buttons on the box.

<img width="1920" height="1208" alt="Triple Passives   Native Transmog" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/ee9f9445-7414-480a-b286-b9b60e1d98fc" />

### Character and boosters (CHARACTER)

- **EDIT CHARACTER STATS:** run with heavy objects, ragdoll on / off, how long a knockdown lasts, no stagger while you hold a melee
  weapon, the MISSION TIMER (on, or off: 600 minutes), the COMMANDO TIMER, Super Destroyer returns in Commando missions, and more.
- **Melee healing:** a melee hit gives back a small share of its damage as health (the gun bash does not count).
- **Boosters** (Armory > Booster): every booster's numbers, editable; booster changes work in missions you host.

### Notices and safety

- **If another mod or HellForge's own editor (F8) changed a number LTEd wanted**, LTEd leaves that number alone and a notice on the ship
  says how many changes did not apply and why. Your F8 edit always wins.
- Before changing anything, LTEd checks every value it expects; a different game build makes it change nothing it does not recognise.
- Everything LTEd changes lives in the game's memory while it runs and is put back when you close the game. Nothing is sent anywhere and
  nothing touches your account.

---

## Hosting and friends

- Spawn, enemy, booster and mission editor changes work in missions **you host**.
- Friends who join you **do not need LTEd** (HellForge never asks for it in its modlist). Their own weapons follow their own game.
- HellForge's offline play is mostly solo; LTEd is made for that first.

## Compatibility

- **Bingus Shared Loader v20:** required.
- **Vanilla Plus Megapack v42** (Bingus's): works alongside it, every option on. Run only one Vanilla Plus.
- **Hell Forge Difficulty:** works with it ON.
- **HellForge's F8 editor:** your F8 edits win; LTEd steps aside and tells you.
- **Other mods that change the same numbers:** LTEd leaves a number another mod changed alone and says so.

## Files it writes

LTEd keeps your edits, progression and settings in its own files in HellForge's userdata folder
(`Documents\HellForge\userdata\environment\Local\CowboyBingus\Helldivers2\Logs\`), and writes a dated log every game start there
(about 0.3 to 1.4 MB a game start).

**WAITING ON HELLFORGE:** LTEd cannot clear its own old logs on HellForge: HellForge's sandbox copies your files back after every session
and never removes any, so a log LTEd deletes there comes back at the next start. Delete old LTEd-v1-*.log files there by hand now and then
(with the game closed); if HellForge ever lets a mod remove its own old files there, LTEd can keep just the newest ones by itself.

## Uninstall

On HellForge's Mods page switch Liber-Tea-Edition off or remove it. Delete the files above to start from LTEd's numbers again.

---

## Every stat change, item by item

The game's number -> Liber-Tea-Edition's. Armour pen is one line with all four angles: straight-on hit / slight angle / steep angle /
glancing. Lines under a bold name belong to that part of the item. A value several items share is listed under each of them.
"LTEd's own copy" = the item got its own copy of a round, damage or blast, so the change touches only that item. "none" = the item did not
have it before; "?" = the game's number is not in our tables; "(x15)" = the same change on 15 parts of that item. The magazine numbers
listed under a gun's part may not reach the game yet on 20 guns: see the KNOWN ISSUE under "A box for every item".

**The design in short.**
- **More enemies, more ammo.** LTEd starts at three times the enemies, so every weapon class carries more spare ammo, and wherever a
  weapon carries more, a resupply refills the same share of it as in the normal game, rounded up.
- **Not everything should explode.** Instead of handing guns explosive rounds, armour penetration and durable damage go up, so ordinary
  guns can do their job against tougher enemies.
- **Every class has a job and a weakness.** Assault rifles are the all-rounders; marksman rifles hit hard and far; SMGs shred anything up
  close but lose most of their punch past about 25 m; shotguns land the biggest single hits, slow what they hit and break big flesh up
  close. A class that is best at everything makes the others pointless.
- **Armour penetration rewards aim.** A raise goes on the straight-on hit while the angled hits stay lower (for example 3/2/2/1): hit armour
  square and you get through, glancing shots still bounce. No gun's armour penetration is generally ever lowered.
- **Durable damage** (the share that hurts big bodies and tough parts) goes to about half of damage on rifles.

## Thanks

- **HellForge** (by SkyeShade): the offline game this runs on, and the Lua bridge LTEd talks to (its kill counts, the melee heal, the
  mission timer freeze). Thank you.
- **Bingus Shared Loader and Vanilla Plus** (by CowboyBingus): LTEd runs through the loader, and its armour camos use the same paint
  system as Match Your Colors.
- **The Helldivers 2 Wiki** contributors (helldivers.wiki.gg): the enemy, structure, weapon, vehicle and mission pictures inside the
  mod, and the data we checked our numbers against. Every picture's source and licence is listed in `Liber-Tea-Edition v0.4.1 - picture
  credits.txt` (most are CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; some are Arrowhead's game art). This mod is free.

==================== LIST 2 OF 4 ====================

LIBER-TEA-EDITION: WEAPON PICTURE CREDITS (THE PROGRESSION WINDOW, THE AFTER-MISSION SCREEN, THE MILESTONE POP-UPS)

Weapon pictures: Helldivers 2 Wiki contributors (helldivers.wiki.gg), the picture at the top of each weapon's page. Each file's own
page below names its uploader and its licence (as the wiki marks it; "unmarked" = no licence tag on the file page). Every picture was
cropped to its visible part, resized and compressed (BC3) by us. Our copies keep the licence of their source; this mod is free
(non-commercial). The enemy pictures are the Archive's. The full list with the uploaders:
